Latest production / packing transaction update: production reporting and packing completion now have their first local and PostgreSQL transaction boundary. `POST /api/production-tasks/{productionTaskId}/report-complete` records a workshop report, treats `qualifiedQty` as the only finished-goods inventory quantity, saves `machineCount` only as machine-cycle evidence, marks the production task complete, moves the order line to `待打包` or `待出库`, increases finished-goods on-hand stock, creates a customer/order-line reservation, writes `production_report` and `production_report_reservation` ledger rows, and optionally creates the packing task. `POST /api/packing-tasks/{packingTaskId}/complete` marks packing complete, creates package rows, moves express/LTL lines to `待打印标签` or `待快运拉走`, writes a zero-quantity `packing_complete` trace ledger, and deliberately does not deduct inventory; deduction remains at fulfillment completion or express/LTL pickup confirmation. Updated files include `server/productionPackingTransactionRepository.mjs`, `server/apiServer.mjs`, `server/authSeed.mjs`, OpenAPI, PostgreSQL live checks, API skeleton checks, and seed checks. Validation passed: `node --check` for touched server/scripts files, `npm run production-packing-transaction:check`, `npm run seed:check`, `npm run api:check`, `npm run api:validate-openapi`, and `npm run db:postgres-live:check`. Follow-up note: office and workshop / packing mobile frontend entries are now wired first-round.

Latest legacy unreserved fulfillment deduction update: fulfillment completion and express/LTL pickup confirmation now support an explicit legacy stock-matching path for old or imported stock lines that lack per-order reservation rows. Normal new orders still deduct through active reservations only; the legacy path only runs when `allowUnreservedInventoryDeduction=true` or `inventoryDeductionPolicy=legacy_stock_match`, the order line is stock/common-goods or printed stock, the finished-goods inventory item is uniquely matched by size / color / handle / style, and inventory is sufficient. It writes `fulfillment_complete_legacy` or `fulfillment_pickup_legacy` ledger rows and, when the old row was visibly already occupied / prepared / waiting pickup, also decreases aggregate reserved stock. `server/apiServer.mjs`, `scripts/check-api-skeleton.mjs`, `scripts/check-fulfillment-action-transaction-repository.mjs`, `scripts/check-postgres-live.mjs`, OpenAPI, the Chinese API draft, product requirements, and `DECISIONS.md` were updated. Validation passed: `node --check` for touched server/scripts files, `npm run fulfillment-action-transaction:check`, `npm run api:check`, `npm run api:validate-openapi`, `npm run db:postgres-live:check`, `npm run fulfillment-api:check`, `npm run build`, and `git diff --check`. PostgreSQL live validation completes old seed fulfillment `F002` with no reservation rows, updates white long-handle inventory from on-hand 2100 / reserved 1200 to on-hand 900 / reserved 0, and writes one `fulfillment_complete_legacy` ledger row. Remaining boundary: production / packing stock movements remain unfinished database transaction work.

Latest order-line quantity adjustment pricing update: formal order-line quantity adjustment now recalculates the receivable amount, creates a new `quantity_adjustment` price snapshot, and synchronizes existing statement-line / statement receivable snapshots in the same local or PostgreSQL transaction boundary. `server/apiServer.mjs` now derives the adjusted amount from the existing order unit price, returns `priceSnapshot`, `finalAmount`, `adjustedStatementLines`, and `adjustedStatements`, and writes the finance-related changed fields into change records and operation logs. `server/orderLineQuantityAdjustmentTransactionRepository.mjs` now persists price snapshots, statement lines, and statement records alongside quantity, fulfillment, reservation, ledger, change-record, and operation-log updates; `server/orderConfirmationTransactionRepository.mjs` now also preserves local confirmation price snapshots. The order-pool API client and UI projection consume the returned final amount so a 500 -> 480 adjustment updates the detail amount from `¥180` to `¥172.8`. Validation passed: `node --check` for touched server/client/scripts files, `npm run order-line-quantity-adjustment-transaction:check`, `npm run api:check`, `npm run order-pool-api:check`, `npm run db:postgres-live:check`, `npm run order-pool-read:check`, `npm run order-confirmation-transaction:check`, `npm run build`, and `git diff --check`. Browser verification at `http://127.0.0.1:5179/` with API `http://127.0.0.1:8795/` adjusted `ORD-0629-001-01` from 500 to 480, confirmed the row quantity changed to 480, the detail amount changed to `¥172.8`, and ERP browser error logs were 0. QA screenshot: `screenshots/p0-order-pool-quantity-adjustment-amount-1280x720.png`. Follow-up note: legacy unreserved fulfillment deduction has since been added; production / packing stock movements remain unfinished database transaction work.

Latest order-pool formal action frontend update: the office order-pool page now wires high-risk formal order-line quantity adjustment and void actions into the existing backend APIs. `src/services/officeOrderPoolApiClient.js` now has POST clients for `POST /api/order-lines/{orderLineId}/quantity-adjustment` and `POST /api/order-lines/{orderLineId}/void`, including Chinese reason-label mapping, backend-denial blocking, and local fallback only for unavailable API. `src/App.jsx` adds a formal action confirmation modal, local quantity / void projections, and API refresh after success; `src/pages/office/index.jsx` replaces the previous placeholder with `调整数量` and `作废正式单` buttons, blocked when the line is closed / cancelled / delivered / already in production. Seed UI permissions now include the order-pool surface for `order.quantity.adjust` and `order.void`. Validation passed: `node --check src/services/officeOrderPoolApiClient.js`, `node --check scripts/check-frontend-order-pool-api-client.mjs`, `npm run order-pool-api:check`, `npm run order-pool-read:check`, `npm run api:check`, `npm run order-line-quantity-adjustment-transaction:check`, `npm run order-line-void-transaction:check`, `npm run build`, and `git diff --check`. Browser verification at `http://127.0.0.1:5174/` with API `http://127.0.0.1:8787/` adjusted `ORD-0629-001-01` from 500 to 480, voided `ORD-0629-001-02`, confirmed the closed line shows `订单已作废`, and confirmed the two formal action buttons are disabled afterward; ERP browser error logs were 0. QA screenshot: `screenshots/p0-order-pool-order-actions-1280x720.png`. Follow-up note: quantity adjustment now recalculates price snapshots / statement amounts; unreserved legacy fulfillment deduction plus production / packing stock movements remain unfinished database transaction work.

Latest order-pool frontend API update: the office order-pool page now reads `GET /api/order-lines` through a dedicated frontend API client and uses `GET /api/order-lines/{id}` for the selected line detail. `src/services/officeOrderPoolApiClient.js` maps OpenAPI order-line fields back to the existing high-density UI shape, keeps custom-print lines visible as `定制印刷`, preserves shorthand display such as `白印黑 / 白袋黑提`, blocks explicit backend denials, and only falls back to the current local list when the API is unavailable. `src/App.jsx` refreshes the order pool on startup, manual refresh, account changes, and successful API confirmation; `src/pages/office/index.jsx` now shows the order-pool data source and can enrich the right detail pane from the detail API. New validation: `npm run order-pool-api:check`. Validation passed: `node --check src/services/officeOrderPoolApiClient.js`, `node --check scripts/check-frontend-order-pool-api-client.mjs`, `npm run order-pool-api:check`, `npm run order-pool-read:check`, `npm run api:check`, `npm run order-api:check`, `npm run build`, and `git diff --check`. Browser verification at `http://127.0.0.1:5174/` with API `http://127.0.0.1:8787/` confirmed `后端订单池 31 行`, a selected custom-print express/LTL line showing `白印黑 / 白袋黑提`, API detail sync, and zero ERP browser errors. QA screenshot: `screenshots/p0-order-pool-api-client-1280x720.png`. Follow-up note: formal order-line void / quantity-adjustment UI actions have since been wired into the order-pool page.

Latest order-pool read model update: order-pool listing and detail reads now have their first repository boundary and PostgreSQL adapter. `server/orderPoolReadRepository.mjs` provides a local-memory adapter that preserves the current P0 workspace behavior and a PostgreSQL adapter selected with `ERP_ORDER_POOL_READ_STORE=postgres` or `ERP_ORDER_STORE=postgres` plus `DATABASE_URL` / `ERP_ORDER_DATABASE_URL`. `GET /api/order-lines` and `GET /api/order-lines/{id}` now read through this repository; the list keeps backward-compatible fields such as `product`, `color`, `status`, and `fulfillment` while also returning OpenAPI-shaped fields such as `productName`, `bagColor`, `lineStatus`, `fulfillmentMethod`, `customerName`, `financeState`, and reservation / fulfillment status summaries. The detail route now returns the OpenAPI-shaped `orderLine`, `originalOrder`, `priceSnapshot`, `inventory`, `fulfillment`, `statement`, `attachments`, and `operationLogs` payload. Docker PostgreSQL live validation now proves the API can read a database-only formal order line that is not present in the API server workspace. New validation: `npm run order-pool-read:check`. Validation passed so far: `node --check` for touched server/scripts files, `npm run order-pool-read:check`, `npm run api:check`, and `npm run db:postgres-live:check`. Remaining boundary: unreserved legacy fulfillment deduction policy is still not defined, and full production / packing stock movements are still not fully database-backed transactions.

Latest fulfillment cancellation transaction update: fulfillment cancellation / rollback release now has its first API and PostgreSQL transaction boundary. `POST /api/fulfillments/{fulfillmentId}/cancel` cancels a not-yet-delivered fulfillment record without closing the order line, releases any active inventory reservation for that order line, decreases `inventory_items.reserved_qty`, writes `inventory_ledger_entries` with `source_type=fulfillment_cancel`, and writes an operation log through `server/fulfillmentActionTransactionRepository.mjs`. The fulfillment action PostgreSQL path now updates both `inventory_reservations.reserved_qty` and `status`. Seed office and management permissions now include `fulfillment.cancel`; warehouse users are explicitly denied cancellation and should continue to use exception reporting for quantity mismatch / unable-to-outbound. OpenAPI is now 40 paths / 99 schemas and includes `CancelFulfillmentRequest` / `CancelFulfillmentResponse`. Docker PostgreSQL live validation covers API order confirmation, quantity adjustment to 8, then fulfillment cancellation and reservation release to zero, including fulfillment status, reservation status/quantity, inventory reserved rollback, `fulfillment_cancel` ledger row, and operation log. Validation passed: `node --check` for touched server/scripts files, `npm run fulfillment-action-transaction:check`, `npm run fulfillment-api:check`, `npm run seed:check`, `npm run api:check`, `npm run api:validate-openapi`, `npm run auth:check`, `npm run db:check`, `npm run db:migrate:dry`, `npm run db:postgres-live:check`, `npm run build`, and `git diff --check`. Follow-up note: order-pool read models now have a first repository boundary; unreserved legacy fulfillment deduction policy and full production / packing stock movements are still not fully database-backed transactions.

Latest order-line quantity adjustment transaction update: formal order-line quantity adjustment now has its first PostgreSQL transaction boundary. `server/orderLineQuantityAdjustmentTransactionRepository.mjs` provides local and PostgreSQL adapters; `POST /api/order-lines/{orderLineId}/quantity-adjustment` now updates a not-yet-produced / not-yet-delivered order line quantity, synchronizes not-yet-delivered fulfillment expected quantity, releases excess reservations on decreases, checks available inventory and adds reservation quantity on increases, writes `inventory_ledger_entries`, writes `order_line_change_records`, and writes `operation_logs` in one transaction. Seed office and management permissions now include `order.quantity.adjust`; warehouse and finance users still cannot adjust formal order quantity. OpenAPI is now 39 paths / 97 schemas and includes `AdjustOrderLineQuantityRequest` / `AdjustOrderLineQuantityResponse`. Docker PostgreSQL live validation covers both direct repository adjustment from 20 to 12 to 15 and API adjustment from 10 to 6 to 8, including order line quantity, fulfillment expected quantity, reservation quantity, inventory reserved quantity, ledger rows, change records, and operation logs. Validation passed: `node --check` for touched server/scripts files, `npm run order-line-quantity-adjustment-transaction:check`, `npm run api:check`, `npm run api:validate-openapi`, `npm run db:postgres-live:check`, `npm run db:check`, `npm run db:migrate:dry`, `npm run auth:check`, `npm run seed:check`, `npm run build`, and `git diff --check`. Remaining boundary: fulfillment cancellation routes still need their own business entry points, unreserved legacy fulfillment deduction policy is still not defined, and order-pool read models plus full production / packing stock movements are still not fully database-backed transactions.

Latest order-line void transaction update: formal order-line void now has its first PostgreSQL transaction boundary. `server/orderLineVoidTransactionRepository.mjs` provides local and PostgreSQL adapters; `POST /api/order-lines/{orderLineId}/void` now closes a not-yet-produced / not-yet-delivered order line, cancels its not-yet-delivered fulfillment records, releases any active reservation for that line, decreases `inventory_items.reserved_qty`, writes an `inventory_ledger_entries` release row, writes an `order_line_change_records` row, and writes an `operation_logs` audit row in one transaction. Seed office and management permissions now include `order.void`; warehouse users still cannot void formal orders. OpenAPI is now 38 paths / 95 schemas and includes `VoidOrderLineRequest` / `VoidOrderLineResponse`; the Chinese API draft documents the side effects. Docker PostgreSQL live validation covers both direct repository void and API void, including confirmation, partial reservation release, remaining reservation release on void, fulfillment cancellation, and inventory reserved rollback. Validation passed: `node --check` for touched server/scripts files, `npm run order-line-void-transaction:check`, `npm run inventory-reservation-release:check`, `npm run order-confirmation-transaction:check`, `npm run fulfillment-action-transaction:check`, `npm run api:check`, `npm run db:postgres-live:check`, `npm run db:check`, `npm run db:migrate:dry`, `npm run api:validate-openapi`, `npm run auth:check`, `npm run seed:check`, `npm run build`, and `git diff --check`. Latest inventory reservation release update: inventory reservation release now has its first PostgreSQL transaction boundary. `server/inventoryReservationReleaseTransactionRepository.mjs` provides local and PostgreSQL adapters; `POST /api/inventory/reservations/{reservationId}/release` now releases all or part of an active reservation, updates `inventory_reservations.reserved_qty/status`, decreases `inventory_items.reserved_qty`, writes a `inventory_ledger_entries` release row, and writes an `operation_logs` audit row in one transaction. Seed permissions now include `inventory.reservation.release` for office, warehouse/outbound, and management users. The OpenAPI draft now uses a dedicated `ReleaseInventoryReservationResponse` that matches the API payload (`qty`, `releasedQty`, `status`, `ledgerId`, `operationLogId`), and the Chinese API draft clarifies that routine fulfillment completion is handled by the fulfillment-action transaction rather than this release route. The Docker PostgreSQL live check covers both direct repository release and API release, including a partial release followed by fulfillment completion against the remaining reserved quantity. Validation passed: `node --check` for touched server/scripts files, `npm run inventory-reservation-release:check`, `npm run api:check`, `npm run db:postgres-live:check`, `npm run fulfillment-action-transaction:check`, `npm run order-confirmation-transaction:check`, `npm run fulfillment-api:check`, `npm run db:check`, `npm run db:migrate:dry`, `npm run api:validate-openapi`, `npm run auth:check`, `npm run seed:check`, `npm run build`, and `git diff --check`. Latest fulfillment inventory transaction update: completing a fulfillment now deducts reserved inventory, releases the matching reservation, and writes the outbound inventory ledger in the same fulfillment-action transaction when an active reservation exists for that order line. `server/fulfillmentActionTransactionRepository.mjs` now accepts `inventoryReservations`, `inventoryAdjustments`, and `inventoryLedgerEntries`; the PostgreSQL path updates `inventory_reservations.status`, adjusts `inventory_items.on_hand_qty` / `reserved_qty`, inserts `inventory_ledger_entries`, and still commits the fulfillment record and operation log together. `server/apiServer.mjs` builds these inventory movements for complete and express/LTL pickup-confirm actions only after the fulfillment reaches `已交付`; older seed fulfillments without reservations are intentionally not auto-deducted. Docker live validation now verifies API order confirmation reserves 10, API fulfillment completion changes real stock from on-hand 2480 / reserved 1330 to on-hand 2470 / reserved 1320, marks the reservation `已出库`, and creates a `fulfillment_complete` ledger row. Validation passed: `node --check` for touched server/scripts files, `npm run fulfillment-action-transaction:check`, `npm run api:check`, `npm run fulfillment-api:check`, `npm run db:postgres-live:check`, `npm run db:check`, `npm run db:migrate:dry`, `npm run api:validate-openapi`, `npm run build`, `git diff --check`, and a trailing-whitespace scan for touched files. Follow-up note: the standalone reservation release transaction is now available for manual / cancellation-style release, but fulfillment/order cancellation routes are not yet wired to it.

Latest fulfillment-action transaction update: outbound / fulfillment actions now have a first PostgreSQL transaction boundary instead of only mutating the API server workspace. `server/fulfillmentActionTransactionRepository.mjs` provides a default local adapter plus a PostgreSQL adapter selected with `ERP_FULFILLMENT_ACTION_TRANSACTION_STORE=postgres`, `ERP_FULFILLMENT_STORE=postgres`, or `DATABASE_URL` / `ERP_FULFILLMENT_DATABASE_URL`; it updates `fulfillment_records` and, depending on the action, writes `print_records`, `fulfillment_exceptions`, `todos`, and `operation_logs` in one transaction. `server/apiServer.mjs` now routes fulfillment print, complete, pickup-confirm, and exception actions through that repository while preserving the P0 prototype behavior. Docker live validation seeds print templates and a warehouse user, then verifies both direct repository writes and the API complete route against real PostgreSQL tables. Validation passed: `npm run fulfillment-action-transaction:check`, `npm run api:check`, `npm run fulfillment-api:check`, `npm run db:check`, `npm run db:postgres-live:check`, and `node --check` for touched server/scripts files. Follow-up note: reserved-inventory deduction / release / ledger entries are now included for completion actions with active reservations; cancellation, unreserved fulfillment policy, order-pool read models, and full production / packing stock movements are still not fully database-backed transactions.

Latest order-confirmation inventory transaction update: order draft confirmation now includes inventory reservation, inventory ledger, and shortage todo side effects in the same PostgreSQL transaction as the formal order rows. `server/orderConfirmationTransactionRepository.mjs` now persists `inventory_reservations`, updates `inventory_items.reserved_qty`, writes `inventory_ledger_entries`, writes shortage `todos`, and writes the confirmation `operation_logs` together with `original_orders`, `order_lines`, `price_snapshots`, and `fulfillment_records`. `server/apiServer.mjs` now constructs those reservation / ledger / todo records and lets the repository commit them instead of directly mutating inventory and todos in the route. Docker live validation seeds minimal `standard_colors` and `inventory_items`, then verifies both direct repository writes and API `POST /api/order-drafts/{draftId}/confirm` create reservation / ledger rows and update real reserved stock. Latest order-confirmation transaction update: order draft confirmation now has its first PostgreSQL transaction boundary for the core order rows. `server/orderConfirmationTransactionRepository.mjs` provides a default local adapter plus a PostgreSQL adapter selected with `ERP_ORDER_CONFIRMATION_TRANSACTION_STORE=postgres`, `ERP_ORDER_STORE=postgres`, or `DATABASE_URL` / `ERP_ORDER_DATABASE_URL`; `server/apiServer.mjs` now sends confirmed `original_orders`, `order_lines`, `price_snapshots`, `fulfillment_records`, and the confirmation `operation_logs` through that repository instead of only mutating the API workspace arrays. Latest statement-preview line persistence update: statement preview generation now persists `statement_lines` in the same PostgreSQL transaction that writes the export file and operation log. `server/statementExportRepository.mjs` accepts the preview lines generated by `server/apiServer.mjs`, replaces the current statement's `statement_lines`, writes `statement_export_files`, and writes `operation_logs` in one transaction when `ERP_STATEMENT_EXPORT_STORE=postgres` or `ERP_STATEMENT_STORE=postgres` is selected. Latest statement-export persistence update: statement export metadata and file content now have a PostgreSQL repository boundary. `server/statementExportRepository.mjs` provides the default local adapter plus a PostgreSQL adapter selected with `ERP_STATEMENT_EXPORT_STORE=postgres` or `ERP_STATEMENT_STORE=postgres`; `server/apiServer.mjs` now routes statement preview export creation, export listing, export download, and latest customer-send export lookup through this repository. Docker live validation now writes and reads statement export files against real PostgreSQL tables, downloads the API workbook from the database-backed route, lists export metadata without content, and verifies mark-sent still links `statement_send_records.export_file_id` to the generated token. Latest statement-send transaction update: statement send marking now has a PostgreSQL transaction boundary. `server/statementSendTransactionRepository.mjs` updates `statements` status / `last_sent_at`, writes `statement_send_records`, and writes `operation_logs` in one transaction; the default local adapter keeps the P0 prototype behavior unchanged. `server/apiServer.mjs` now routes `POST /api/statements/{statementId}/mark-sent` through this repository and links the latest customer-send export token into `statement_send_records.export_file_id` when available. Latest payment-record repository update: statement payment recording now has its first repository boundary instead of only mutating the API server workspace array. `server/paymentRecordRepository.mjs` provides the default `local_memory` adapter plus a PostgreSQL adapter selected with `ERP_PAYMENT_RECORD_STORE=postgres` or `ERP_STATEMENT_STORE=postgres` and `DATABASE_URL` / `ERP_PAYMENT_DATABASE_URL`; `POST /api/statements/{statementId}/payments` now writes payment summaries through that repository. `scripts/check-postgres-live.mjs` seeds the required customers, statements, and users, then validates both direct PostgreSQL `payment_records` writes and the payment API route against a temporary Docker `postgres:16-alpine` database. Latest PostgreSQL live-check update: the attachment database path is no longer SQL-boundary-only. `scripts/check-postgres-live.mjs` now starts a temporary Docker `postgres:16-alpine` database, applies all 4 migrations, seeds the minimal users required by attachment foreign keys, executes the PostgreSQL attachment repository and access-audit repository against real tables, then starts the API server with those PostgreSQL repositories and verifies `POST /api/attachments`, owner-scoped listing, content read, access-url creation, and access-log querying. This live check exposed and fixed two real PostgreSQL issues: `buildInsertAttachmentSql(...)` now returns owner metadata from the `inserted_link` CTE instead of reading `attachment_links` through a stale statement snapshot, and both PostgreSQL repository JSON runners now ignore `psql` command tags such as `INSERT 0 1` before parsing JSON. New command: `npm run db:postgres-live:check`. Latest payment-proof access-audit visibility update: the P0 payment-proof viewer now shows recent attachment access audit records after a proof is opened. `src/App.jsx` calls `listOfficeAttachmentAccessLogs(...)` when loading or reopening a payment screenshot, stores the returned audit summary with the selected statement attachment, and renders action, operator, access mode, and time in `AttachmentViewerModal`. `src/styles.css` adds the compact audit section and reduces the viewer image area so the dialog still fits operational desktop viewports. Validation passed: `npm run build`, `npm run attachment-api:check`, `npm run api:check`, and `git diff --check`. Browser verification used isolated API `http://127.0.0.1:8793/`, isolated frontend `http://127.0.0.1:5178/`, and temporary storage `.erp-local-storage/browser-audit-ui`; after registering `payment-proof-access-audit-smoke.svg` for `ST-0629-001` and generating an access URL, the payment-proof viewer showed `最近 2 条 / 共 2 条` with `读取内容` and `生成访问地址`, ERP browser errors were zero, and the QA screenshot is `screenshots/p0-payment-proof-access-audit-viewer-1280x720.png`. Remaining boundary: production object storage, persisted thumbnails, live PostgreSQL access-audit writes, lifecycle rules, and finer owner-scoped authorization are still not connected.

Latest attachment access-audit persistence update: attachment access audit now has its own repository boundary instead of relying only on in-memory `operationLogs`. `server/attachmentAccessAuditRepository.mjs` defaults to `.erp-local-storage/metadata/attachment-access-logs.json` and also exposes a PostgreSQL SQL boundary for `attachment_access_logs`; `server/apiServer.mjs` records a separate `ALOG-*` access-audit ID while retaining the related `operationLogId`, so server restart no longer loses local access audit history. The migration draft now includes `attachment_access_logs`, bringing the database draft to 47 core tables. `GET /api/attachments/{attachmentId}/access-logs` still requires `attachment.view` and now reads through the repository. Validation passed: `npm run attachment-repository:check`, `npm run api:check`, `npm run attachment-api:check`, and `npm run db:check`. Remaining boundary: PostgreSQL access-audit writes are SQL-boundary checked only because no live database URL was provided; live object-storage credentials, persistent thumbnails, lifecycle rules, object-storage event reconciliation, and production credential handling are still pending.

Latest S3-compatible attachment object-storage configuration update: `ERP_ATTACHMENT_OBJECT_STORAGE=object_storage` is no longer only a hard failure placeholder. `server/attachmentObjectStorage.mjs` now resolves S3-compatible configuration from explicit options or environment variables, including endpoint, bucket, region, access key, secret key, optional session token, key prefix, and path-style mode. When configured, the adapter signs zero-dependency AWS Signature V4 `PUT` / `GET` requests, stores uploaded content under object keys such as `attachments/{attachmentId}/{fileName}`, reads objects through signed backend proxy requests, and returns direct signed object-storage GET URLs from the existing access-url boundary. When required config is missing, it still returns an explicit unconfigured placeholder instead of silently pretending to work. `server/apiServer.mjs` now awaits attachment object writes and reads so real network object storage can be used later. Validation passed: `npm run attachment-storage:check`, `npm run api:check`, `npm run attachment-api:check`, `npm run api:validate-openapi`, `npm run build`, `npm run db:check`, `npm run attachment-repository:check`, and `git diff --check`. Remaining boundary: this has not been tested against a live S3/OSS/COS bucket, credentials are not configured in this repo, thumbnails/lifecycle/access-audit are still pending, and the default runtime remains `local_fs`.

Latest attachment object-storage boundary update: attachment bytes now go through an object-storage interface instead of local-file helpers inside `server/apiServer.mjs`. `server/attachmentObjectStorage.mjs` defines the default `local_fs` adapter for `putObject`, `readObject`, short-lived access URL creation, token validation, safe storage-key naming, and legacy data-URL fallback. `server/apiServer.mjs` now creates `workspace.attachmentObjectStorage`, reports it in `/api/health`, uses it when `POST /api/attachments` persists uploaded content, uses it for `GET /api/attachments/{attachmentId}/access-url`, and uses it for content reads. Missing `ERP_ATTACHMENT_OBJECT_STORAGE=object_storage` configuration now fails explicitly, while configured S3-compatible signing is covered by the later object-storage configuration update. New validation: `npm run attachment-storage:check`; API health checks now confirm the default `local_json` repository plus `local_fs` object storage. Validation passed: `npm run attachment-storage:check`, `npm run api:check`, `npm run attachment-api:check`, `npm run api:validate-openapi`, `npm run build`, `npm run db:check`, `npm run attachment-repository:check`, and `git diff --check`. Remaining boundary: default runtime is still local filesystem storage, and live S3/OSS/COS credentials, persistent thumbnails, object-storage lifecycle rules, owner-level access audit, and production credential handling remain pending.

Latest attachment access-url update: P0 attachments now have a first signed-access URL boundary instead of only direct authenticated content reads. `server/apiServer.mjs` adds `GET /api/attachments/{attachmentId}/access-url`, guarded by `attachment.view`, returning a short-lived API-proxy URL with `accessToken`, `expiresAt`, TTL, delivery mode, storage provider, file name, and content type. `GET /api/attachments/{attachmentId}/content` now accepts a valid non-expired access token even when the request has no action permissions, and rejects expired or invalid tokens with `ATTACHMENT_ACCESS_TOKEN_INVALID`. `src/services/officeAttachmentApiClient.js` adds `createOfficeAttachmentAccessUrl(...)` and normalizes `/api/...` relative URLs without double-prefixing the API base. The OpenAPI draft now has 36 paths and 90 schemas. Validation passed: `npm run attachment-api:check`, `npm run api:validate-openapi`, `npm run api:check`, `npm run build`, `npm run db:check`, `npm run attachment-repository:check`, and `git diff --check`. Remaining boundary: this is still an API-proxy token over local files, not real object-storage signed URLs, persisted thumbnail generation, owner-level content authorization beyond the first view permission check, or database-backed access audit records.

Latest attachment repository-boundary update: attachment metadata now sits behind a repository interface instead of being hardwired directly into `server/apiServer.mjs`. `server/attachmentRepository.mjs` provides the default `local_json` repository for `.erp-local-storage/metadata/attachment-records.json` plus an explicit `postgres` adapter selected with `ERP_ATTACHMENT_STORE=postgres` and `DATABASE_URL` or `ERP_ATTACHMENT_DATABASE_URL`. `POST /api/attachments`, `GET /api/attachments`, and `GET /api/attachments/{attachmentId}/content` now call that repository while keeping local file bytes under `.erp-local-storage/attachments/`. The PostgreSQL adapter targets the formal `attachments` and `attachment_links` tables, including storage provider/key, digest, thumbnail, metadata, and signed URL expiry fields. `AttachmentSummary` and the frontend attachment client now carry thumbnail and signed-URL expiry metadata. Validation passed: `npm run attachment-repository:check`, `npm run attachment-api:check`, `npm run api:validate-openapi`, `npm run api:check`, `npm run build`, `npm run db:check`, and `git diff --check`. Remaining boundary: PostgreSQL execution is covered by SQL-boundary tests only because no live database URL was provided; object storage, signed URL generation, persistent thumbnails, owner-level content authorization, and cross-business database transactions are still not connected.

Latest formal attachment-table draft update: the database migration draft now has a PostgreSQL-ready attachment metadata shape instead of only a URL placeholder. `db/migrations/0004_statements_todos_audit.sql` extends `attachments` with MIME type, file size, content flag, storage provider/key/url, content reference, SHA-256 digest, thumbnail storage/url, signed URL expiry, metadata JSON, and update time. `attachment_links` now uses `owner_type` / `owner_id` / `purpose`, matching the owner-scoped attachment API. Attachment owner, attachment id, purpose/status, upload time, and storage-provider/key indexes were added. `scripts/dbMigrationUtils.mjs` now validates required attachment and attachment-link columns, so `npm run db:check` fails if future drafts drop object-storage or thumbnail fields. The ERD, migration runner notes, and decisions were aligned. Validation passed: `npm run db:check` and `npm run db:migrate:dry`. Remaining boundary: this is still a migration draft and dry-run only; the API server still writes attachments to memory/local files/local JSON, not PostgreSQL transactions, object storage, or signed URLs.

Latest payment-proof download update: P0 payment-proof viewer now supports downloading the currently loaded preview image. `src/App.jsx` adds a preview-download helper, derives the download file name from the attachment file name first, then `content-disposition`, then `attachmentId`, and sanitizes invalid filename characters. `AttachmentViewerModal` now shows a `下载原图` action that downloads the loaded data URL and reports the downloaded file name in the toast. Validation passed: `npm run build`, `npm run attachment-api:check`, and `git diff --check`. Browser verification used an isolated API server on `http://127.0.0.1:8792/`, an isolated frontend on `http://127.0.0.1:5177/`, and a temporary attachment storage root; after registering `payment-proof-download-smoke.svg` for `ST-0629-001`, the statement page opened the payment-proof viewer, the `下载原图` button was enabled, clicking it reported `已下载付款凭证：payment-proof-download-smoke.svg。`, and ERP browser errors were zero. Latest statement payment-proof hydration update: P0 statement payment evidence now auto-hydrates from the shared attachment list when the office user opens the statement/payment page and selects a statement. `src/App.jsx` calls `listOfficeAttachments(...)` with `ownerType=statement`, the selected statement ID, `purpose=payment_screenshot`, and `fileType=image`, then syncs returned attachment summaries into that statement's `paymentAttachmentFiles`. `src/state/officeStatementActions.js` adds `syncStatementPaymentAttachments(...)`, preserving already-loaded preview data URLs, preview status, and content type when backend summaries are refreshed. `scripts/check-frontend-attachment-api-client.mjs` now asserts that listed attachments sync into statement payment evidence and that resyncing does not drop a loaded preview. Latest frontend fulfillment API-client update: P0 outbound / fulfillment actions now use a switchable API client instead of only mutating local frontend state. New `src/services/officeFulfillmentApiClient.js` calls `POST /api/fulfillments/{id}/print`, `/complete`, `/pickup-confirm`, and `/exception`, mapping office-facing document types, print actions, and exception reasons into backend enum values. Explicit backend denials remain blocked and do not fall back; only network unavailability falls back to the prior local prototype flow. `src/App.jsx` now treats fulfillment buttons and modal confirmation as async API-first actions, then applies the existing local projection for the P0 UI; `标记已备货` remains a light local-only state with an explicit toast. New validation script: `npm run fulfillment-api:check`, covering print, complete, pickup confirmation, exception submission, permission-denial blocking, and network fallback. `scripts/check-api-skeleton.mjs` now also asserts the fulfillment complete route. Validation passed: `npm run fulfillment-api:check`, `npm run api:check`, `npm run api:validate-openapi`, `npm run auth:check`, `npm run order-api:check`, `npm run inventory-api:check`, `npm run todo-api:check`, `npm run seed:check`, and `npm run build`. Browser verification at `http://127.0.0.1:5174/` with a temporary API server at `http://127.0.0.1:8787/` confirmed backend-authenticated office fulfillment printing for the White Whale express/LTL row, pickup confirmation into delivered state, and a Zhangsan pickup quantity mismatch with actual quantity 480 creating a shared office todo; ERP browser error logs were empty. QA screenshot: `screenshots/p0-fulfillment-api-client-1280x720.png`. Remaining boundary: real printer/templates, delivery evidence/signatures/photos, inventory-ledger persistence, and statement integration are not fully connected yet.

Latest frontend inventory-correction API-client update: P0 inventory correction draft creation now uses a switchable API client and the backend skeleton has the matching in-memory write routes. New `src/services/officeInventoryApiClient.js` calls `POST /api/inventory/correction-drafts`, maps office-facing Chinese reason labels to the OpenAPI enum, blocks explicit backend denials instead of falling back, and only creates a local pending draft when the API is unavailable. `server/apiServer.mjs` now supports creating and confirming inventory correction drafts: creation returns `待确认生效`, a todo, and an operation log without changing inventory totals; confirmation requires `inventory.correction.confirm`, writes a correction ledger entry, and only then updates on-hand inventory. Seed permissions were aligned so office and warehouse/outbound users can create correction drafts while management can confirm them. `src/App.jsx` owns the async inventory correction action, and `src/pages/office/index.jsx` only collects the actual count and displays the returned draft. New validation script: `npm run inventory-api:check`, covering request mapping, bearer/header auth, permission-denial blocking, and network fallback. `scripts/check-api-skeleton.mjs` now asserts finance denial, warehouse draft creation without inventory mutation, warehouse confirmation denial, and management confirmation with inventory-ledger creation. Validation passed: `npm run inventory-api:check`, `npm run api:check`, `npm run seed:check`, `npm run api:validate-openapi`, `npm run auth:check`, `npm run order-api:check`, `npm run todo-api:check`, `npm run build`, and `git diff --check`. Browser verification at `http://127.0.0.1:5174/` with a temporary API server at `http://127.0.0.1:8787/` confirmed backend-authenticated office inventory correction draft creation for system 2480 / actual 2490 / diff 10; the inventory table still showed on-hand 2480 after draft creation and ERP browser error logs were empty. QA screenshot: `screenshots/p0-inventory-correction-api-client-1280x720.png`. Remaining boundary: the frontend only creates correction drafts; confirmation is covered by backend/API checks but still lacks a management review UI and database transaction persistence.

Latest frontend todo API-client update: P0 shared-todo handling actions now use a switchable API client instead of only mutating local frontend state. New `src/services/officeTodoApiClient.js` calls `POST /api/todos/{todoId}/handle` for handled / viewed / print-label actions, snooze reminders, reopen, management-view logging, and low-risk batch print confirmation. Explicit backend denials such as permission failures or validation errors stay blocked and do not fall back; only network unavailability falls back to the prior local prototype flow. `src/App.jsx` now treats shared-todo write-state branches as async API-backed actions, while navigation-only actions still remain local page routing. `server/apiServer.mjs` now maps todo records with `reminder` / `lastAction` snooze state to `snoozed` so API responses match the local helper behavior. New validation script: `npm run todo-api:check`, covering action mapping, single-todo handling, batch handling, permission-denial blocking, and network fallback. `scripts/check-api-skeleton.mjs` now also asserts todo reopen and snooze behavior. Validation passed: `npm run todo-api:check`, `npm run api:check`, `npm run api:validate-openapi`, `npm run auth:check`, `npm run order-api:check`, `npm run build`, and `git diff --check`. Browser verification at `http://127.0.0.1:5174/` with a temporary API server at `http://127.0.0.1:8787/` confirmed backend-authenticated office todo snooze, handled, reopen, and low-risk batch print actions; the todo badge moved 8 -> 7 -> 8 -> 7 as expected, and ERP browser error logs were empty. QA screenshot: `screenshots/p0-todo-api-client-1280x720.png`. Remaining boundary: todo handling only records todo state and operation logs; stock, money, order, and fulfillment side effects must still go through their own business APIs.

Latest frontend order-draft save/confirm API-client update: the P0 order-entry `保存草稿` and `保存并确认` actions now use the switchable order-draft API client instead of only mutating local frontend state. `src/services/officeOrderApiClient.js` now covers `/api/order-drafts/recognize`, `PATCH /api/order-drafts/{draftId}`, and `POST /api/order-drafts/{draftId}/confirm`, reusing backend `draftId` / `clientRevision` across recognition, save, and confirmation. API save success maps backend todo summaries back into frontend todo inputs; explicit backend denials such as permission failures or business conflicts stay blocked and do not fall back; only network unavailability falls back to the prior local prototype flow. `src/App.jsx` now treats order-entry actions as async and runs backend confirmation before applying the existing local order-pool projection, because the current API skeleton returns OpenAPI summaries rather than the full frontend row shape. `scripts/check-frontend-order-api-client.mjs` covers recognition, save, confirm, draft-line field mapping, permission-denial blocking, and network fallback. `scripts/check-api-skeleton.mjs` now also asserts `PATCH /api/order-drafts/{draftId}` saves draft status, increments revision, returns a todo, and writes an operation log. Validation passed: `npm run order-api:check`, `npm run auth:check`, `npm run api:check`, `npm run api:validate-openapi`, `npm run build`, and `git diff --check`. Browser verification at `http://127.0.0.1:5174/` with a temporary API server at `http://127.0.0.1:8787/` confirmed backend-authenticated office recognition, draft save, and save-and-confirm for `王五包装 30*38红10个 明天自提`; the todo badge increased from 8 to 9, confirmation navigated to the order pool with a new `ORD-P0-*` line, and ERP browser error logs were empty. QA screenshot: `screenshots/p0-order-save-confirm-api-client-1280x720.png`. Remaining boundary: backend writes are still in-memory seed writes, and the frontend still uses local state projection after successful confirmation until list/detail APIs return the full frontend-ready shape.

Latest frontend order-recognition API-client update: the P0 order-entry `识别` action now uses a switchable API client instead of only calling the local parser. `src/services/officeOrderApiClient.js` calls `/api/order-drafts/recognize` with the current seed bearer token when available, or `x-erp-user-id` otherwise. Successful API responses are mapped back into the existing editable frontend draft rows; network failures fall back to local `parseOrderText`, while explicit API denials such as 401/403 do not fall back and therefore do not bypass backend permissions. To preserve multi-customer pasted messages, `server/apiServer.mjs` and `docs/development/erp-api-openapi-draft.yaml` now include optional `customerId` and `customerName` on recognized draft lines, and `scripts/check-api-skeleton.mjs` asserts those fields. New validation script: `npm run order-api:check`, covering API recognition mapping, permission-denial blocking, and local fallback. Validation passed: `npm run order-api:check`, `npm run auth:check`, `npm run api:check`, `npm run api:validate-openapi`, `npm run build`, and `git diff --check`. Browser verification at `http://127.0.0.1:5174/` with a temporary API server at `http://127.0.0.1:8787/` confirmed backend-authenticated office recognition for a two-customer sample, preserved row customers `张三服饰` and `王五包装`, showed an API-recognition toast, and produced zero ERP browser errors. QA screenshot: `screenshots/p0-order-recognition-api-client-1280x720.png`. This recognition client is now reused by the order-draft save/confirm client described above.

Latest frontend auth-service update: the P0 office frontend now has an API-shaped seed authentication client in `src/services/officeAuthService.js`. On startup, `src/App.jsx` attempts to restore the current seed session through `/api/auth/me`; when the top-bar account selector changes, it immediately applies local seed permissions for responsiveness, then calls `/api/auth/login` and refreshes permissions from the API response. The top bar now distinguishes `后端认证` from `本地权限`, and the client falls back to local seed permissions if the API is unavailable so the prototype remains usable. `npm run auth:check` covers API login request shape, seed-session storage/restore, and local fallback behavior. Validation passed: `npm run auth:check`, `npm run api:check`, and `npm run build`. Browser verification at `http://127.0.0.1:5174/` with a temporary API server at `http://127.0.0.1:8787/` confirmed finance API-authenticated permissions disable new-order and order-entry actions, warehouse API-authenticated permissions disable statement/payment actions, and ERP browser error logs were empty. QA screenshot: `screenshots/p0-front-auth-api-session-1280x720.png`. This is still seed-only auth, not database-backed accounts, password hashing, server-side session storage, token revocation, or persisted RBAC.

Latest frontend permission-guard update: the P0 office frontend now consumes a local seed permission context for the current account and key button guards. The top bar can switch between office, warehouse/outbound, finance/statement, management, and driver seed accounts; high-risk buttons for new order creation, order recognition/save/confirmation, inventory correction draft creation, fulfillment print/complete/pickup/exception, statement send/payment/variance/write-off, and shared-todo handling are disabled when the current account lacks the mapped permission, with the missing permission shown in the button title. Operator-facing messages now use the selected account instead of always saying `办公室A`. Browser verification at `http://127.0.0.1:5174/` confirmed that finance cannot perform order entry but can operate statement/payment actions, warehouse cannot operate statement/payment actions but can operate fulfillment actions, and driver cannot perform high-risk fulfillment actions; ERP browser error logs were empty. QA screenshot: `screenshots/p0-front-permission-guard-1280x720.png`.

Latest backend auth-session update: the Node.js API skeleton now includes seed login, current-session lookup, and logout placeholders. `POST /api/auth/login` authenticates a seed login name / password and returns an HMAC-signed bearer token; `GET /api/auth/me` verifies the `seed-session` token and returns the current session plus effective permissions; `POST /api/auth/logout` currently returns a stateless logout placeholder because the seed token has no server-side revocation store yet. `docs/development/erp-api-openapi-draft.yaml` now includes the authentication endpoints, bringing the draft to 32 paths, 86 schemas, and 11 tags. `scripts/check-api-skeleton.mjs` covers anonymous `/api/auth/me` denial, invalid password denial, finance seed login, and token-backed current-session permission lookup. This is still not database-backed accounts, password hashing, server-side session storage, token revocation, or RBAC persistence. Validation passed: `npm run api:check` and `npm run api:validate-openapi`.

Latest backend seed-auth update: the Node.js API skeleton now has a multi-account seed permission context instead of a single hardcoded office user. `server/authSeed.mjs` defines office, warehouse/outbound, finance/statement, management, and driver seed users, then composes `buttonPermissions`, `actionPermissions`, and grant summaries from role defaults. `GET /api/permissions/effective` can now read the current seed user from `x-erp-user-id` or `Authorization: Bearer seed:<userId>`, with unknown users returning a disabled empty-permission context. `scripts/check-api-skeleton.mjs` verifies the default office positive flow, finance denial for order recognition, warehouse denial for statement payment recording, and unknown-user empty permissions; `scripts/check-seed-data.mjs` also checks the seed permission matrix. This is still not real login/session/password/token verification or database-backed RBAC. Validation passed: `npm run api:check`, `npm run api:validate-openapi`, `npm run db:check`, `npm run db:migrate:dry`, `npm run seed:check`, `npm run build`, and `git diff --check`.

Latest backend response-shape update: the first Node.js API skeleton responses are now closer to the OpenAPI contract. `server/apiServer.mjs` maps order-confirmation output into contract-shaped order-line summaries, price snapshots, inventory checks, reservation summaries, fulfillment task summaries, and todo summaries; fulfillment lists now include metrics and contract-shaped list items; print responses now return the required print-record fields; fulfillment completion / pickup responses now return a boolean `statementCandidate`; statement write-off responses now include receivable, received, variance, and debt amount; todo handling returns contract-shaped todo list items. `docs/development/erp-api-openapi-draft.yaml` now includes the current skeleton error codes such as `PERMISSION_DENIED` and `VALIDATION_ERROR`. `scripts/check-api-skeleton.mjs` now asserts the response-shape fields and covers print, express/LTL pickup confirmation, and statement write-off in addition to the prior read/write smoke flow. Validation passed: `npm run api:check`, `npm run api:validate-openapi`, and `npm run build`.

Latest backend permission-guard update: the Node.js API skeleton now checks action permissions before the first in-memory write endpoints run. `server/apiServer.mjs` guards order-draft recognition / save / confirmation, fulfillment exception / print / complete / express-LTL pickup confirmation, statement send / payment / variance / write-off, and todo handling against `actionPermissions`, returning `403 PERMISSION_DENIED` with `requiredPermission` when the current permission context is missing the action. `server/seedData.mjs` now includes the first office-account action permissions, and `scripts/check-api-skeleton.mjs` verifies both the default positive write flow and a negative `x-erp-action-permissions: none` case. This is still seed-based permission checking, not real login/session/database-backed RBAC. Validation passed: `npm run api:check` and `npm run api:validate-openapi`.

Latest backend write-API update: the Node.js API skeleton now includes the first in-memory write endpoints instead of being read-only only. `server/apiServer.mjs` supports order-draft recognition, draft save, draft confirmation into formal order lines, fulfillment exception reporting, fulfillment print / complete / express-LTL pickup confirmation, statement mark-sent, payment recording, variance handling, write-off checks, todo handling, and operation-log querying. These writes mutate only the current server process workspace and append in-memory `operationLogs`; they are not database-backed yet. `scripts/check-api-skeleton.mjs` now performs a read/write smoke test covering recognition, order confirmation, fulfillment exception, payment recording, variance handling, todo handling, and operation-log lookup. Validation passed: `npm run api:check`.

Latest backend migration-runner update: a zero-dependency migration runner has been added. `scripts/dbMigrationUtils.mjs` centralizes migration loading, order validation, core-table coverage checks, and SHA-256 checksums. `scripts/run-db-migrations.mjs` supports `npm run db:migrate:dry` for a no-database execution plan and `DATABASE_URL=... npm run db:migrate` for applying pending migrations through the local `psql` client while recording them in `schema_migrations`. The runner refuses checksum mismatches for already-applied migration IDs. Documentation lives at `docs/development/database-migration-runner.zh-CN.md`. Validation passed: `npm run db:check` and `npm run db:migrate:dry`.

Latest backend migration/seed update: the ERD draft has been split into the first PostgreSQL migration draft under `db/migrations/`. The four migration files cover users/roles/permissions, customers/master data, order drafts/formal order lines, price snapshots, finished-goods inventory, reservations, ledger entries, inventory correction drafts, production tasks, workshop reports, packing tasks, packages, fulfillment records/exceptions, print templates/records, statements, statement lines/sends, payments, variance records, todos, attachments, operation logs, after-sales records, and responsibility clues. `scripts/check-db-migrations.mjs` and `npm run db:check` validate migration order, prevent destructive `DROP TABLE`, and confirm 46 required core tables. Seed data has also moved behind `server/seeds/syntheticOfficeSeed.mjs`, with `server/seeds/README.zh-CN.md`, `scripts/check-seed-data.mjs`, and `npm run seed:check` validating the synthetic workspace bridge. Validation passed: `npm run db:check`, `npm run seed:check`, `npm run api:validate-openapi`, `npm run api:check`, and `npm run build`.

Latest backend skeleton update: the first zero-dependency Node.js API skeleton has been added under `server/`, with validation/check scripts under `scripts/`. `server/apiServer.mjs` exposes read-only seed routes for health, OpenAPI status, office workspace, order lines, inventory items, fulfillments, statement customers, todos, and effective permissions. `server/openapiValidation.mjs` validates the OpenAPI YAML using the same Ruby YAML/ref-check approach as the prior manual checks, and `server/seedData.mjs` adapts the existing P0 fixtures into backend seed responses. New package scripts are `npm run api:dev`, `npm run api:check`, and `npm run api:validate-openapi`; `docs/development/backend-minimal-skeleton.zh-CN.md` documents the skeleton boundaries and routes. Validation passed: `npm run api:validate-openapi`, `npm run api:check`, `npm run build`, and `git diff --check`.

Latest Stage 3 ERD/table-structure update: `docs/development/erp-erd-draft.zh-CN.md` now defines the first V1 ERD and table-structure draft. It covers users/roles/permissions, customers and master data, order drafts, original orders and order lines, price snapshots, finished-goods inventory, inventory reservations and ledgers, inventory correction drafts, production/packing/package placeholders, fulfillment records, print records/templates, statements, statement lines, sends, payments, variance records, todos, attachments, operation logs, after-sales records, and responsibility clues. It also records first-pass unique/index rules, API-to-table mapping, migration ordering, and out-of-scope items for the first core backend. `00_项目入口.md`, `docs/development/erp-data-api-draft.zh-CN.md`, and `docs/development/code-development-plan.zh-CN.md` now link to the ERD draft. The OpenAPI contract now covers the main P0 office API surface for recognition, order pool, inventory, fulfillment, statement/payment, attachments, todos, permissions, and logs. `docs/development/erp-data-api-draft.zh-CN.md` and `docs/development/code-development-plan.zh-CN.md` have been updated; the recommended next step is moving from API-contract drafting into first ERD/table-structure drafting, backend stack selection, local seed data, and real-sample fixture switching.

Latest Stage 3 OpenAPI statement/fulfillment expansion: `docs/development/erp-api-openapi-draft.yaml` now has 19 paths and 56 schemas. The OpenAPI error model now covers fulfillment readiness, print-before-complete requirements, pickup confirmation constraints, duplicate statement sending, statement preview readiness, and payment amount/permission failures. `docs/development/erp-data-api-draft.zh-CN.md` and `docs/development/code-development-plan.zh-CN.md` have been updated to reflect the 19-path contract and move the next API work to order recognition/save-draft, inventory list/correction-draft creation, todo handling, statement write-off, and permission skeletons.

Latest Stage 3 OpenAPI expansion: `docs/development/erp-api-openapi-draft.yaml` has been extended from 5 to 12 paths and from 22 to 40 schemas. Latest Stage 3 OpenAPI update: the first machine-readable API contract draft now lives at `docs/development/erp-api-openapi-draft.yaml`. It is an OpenAPI 3.1 draft with 5 paths and 22 schemas covering order draft confirmation, inventory reservation, inventory reservation release, fulfillment exception reporting, and statement variance handling. `docs/development/erp-data-api-draft.zh-CN.md` now links the OpenAPI file and keeps the Chinese business explanation as the source for process meaning. `docs/development/code-development-plan.zh-CN.md` now marks the first OpenAPI conversion as complete and moves the next API work to order-pool query/detail, inventory correction confirmation, print/reprint, attachment upload, and operation logs. Validation passed: Ruby YAML parsing confirmed OpenAPI `3.1.0`, 5 paths, and 22 schemas; `npm run build` passes; `git diff --check` has no output.

Latest Stage 3 API-contract update: the first field-level API contract pass is now documented in `docs/development/erp-data-api-draft.zh-CN.md`. It covers four high-risk flows in request / response / error / permission-adjacent terms: order draft confirmation, inventory reservation / release, fulfillment exceptions, and statement variance handling. `src/services/officeMockService.js` has also been expanded from workspace loading into a local API-shaped boundary for draft-save todo creation, draft confirmation, fulfillment action updates, fulfillment-exception todo creation, statement sent marking, and statement write-off confirmation. `src/App.jsx` now calls these service wrappers for those flows while retaining React state wiring and modal orchestration. This keeps P0 frontend behavior local, but moves the code closer to the future real HTTP API replacement path. `npm run build` passes; Node service validation covered workspace loading, draft-save todo creation, sample draft confirmation into one formal line and one fulfillment task, fulfillment action wrapping, exception todo creation, statement sent marking, and statement write-off wrapping. Browser verification at `http://127.0.0.1:5174/` covered reload, default shared-todo badge 8, order-entry recognition for `王五包装 30*38红10个 今天自提`, save-and-confirm into the order pool as `ORD-P0-032-01`, and zero ERP browser error logs. Latest Stage 2 fixture-scenario update: P0 fake data now has a named scenario catalog and factory in `src/data/fixtures.js`. `officeScenarioDefinitions` defines six acceptance-oriented scenarios: P0 office baseline, order-entry shorthand parsing, fulfillment express/exception handling, statement underpayment, inventory shortage/correction, and shared-todo priority handling. `createOfficeScenarioData(...)` now returns cloned customers, order lines, inventories, todos, fulfillments, statements, sample text, and scenario metadata so future scenario switching or real-sample fixtures can avoid mutating shared base arrays. `src/App.jsx` now loads data through this factory, passes scenario customers/sample text through page helpers, and `src/pages/office/index.jsx` no longer imports default customers/sample text directly. `confirmDraftOrder(...)` now receives the active scenario customers instead of reading the default fixtures table. `npm run build` passes; a Node fixture validation verified 6 scenarios, all focus IDs, and clone isolation; browser verification at `http://127.0.0.1:5174/` covered the scenario toast, nav badge 8, all six page titles, order-entry sample shorthand, six parsed draft rows, and zero ERP browser error logs. QA screenshot: `screenshots/p0-fixture-scenarios-smoke.png`.

Latest Stage 2 modal-actions update: modal confirmation routing now lives in `src/state/officeModalActions.js`. The helper owns modal titles, default input values, default reasons, shared reason options, and the confirmation dispatcher for fulfillment exceptions, fulfillment print previews, statement payment registration, statement variance handling, and statement preview confirmation. `src/App.jsx` now closes the modal, calls `confirmOfficeModal(...)`, and applies returned fulfillment / statement / todo / toast changes instead of carrying a long modal-type branch; `App.jsx` is down to 793 lines. `npm run build` passes; browser verification at `http://127.0.0.1:5174/` covered express/LTL print modal to `待确认拉走`, quantity-mismatch modal creating an office todo, 李四电商 payment-registration modal, 李四电商 variance-handling modal to `有欠款`, nav-badge updates, and zero ERP browser error logs. QA screenshot: `screenshots/p0-modal-actions-extracted-smoke.png`.

Latest Stage 2 todo-state update: shared office todo state transitions now have a dedicated helper module in `src/state/officeTodoActions.js`. Mark-handled, reopen, low-risk batch print handling, snooze reminders, management-view acknowledgement, and customer-pending updates have moved out of `src/App.jsx`. `App.jsx` still owns navigation routing and React state writes, while the helper returns deterministic todo-list transformations and reminder labels. `npm run build` passes; browser verification at `http://127.0.0.1:5174/` covered marking a todo handled, 30-minute snooze reminder, low-risk batch print handling, switching to handled todos, reopening a handled todo, nav-badge/stat updates, and zero ERP browser error logs. QA screenshot: `screenshots/p0-todo-state-actions-extracted-smoke.png`.

Latest Stage 2 order-save-state update: order-entry save / confirmation state transitions now have a dedicated helper module in `src/state/officeOrderActions.js`. Draft-save todo payload creation, formal order-line generation, fulfillment-task generation, inventory-reservation calculation, and shortage-todo payload creation have moved out of `src/App.jsx`. `App.jsx` now delegates the business result calculation to `confirmDraftOrder(...)`, then only applies React state updates for draft rows, order lines, fulfillments, inventory records, selected order, active page, and toast. `npm run build` passes; browser verification at `http://127.0.0.1:5174/` covered a stock order entry `王五包装 30*38红10个 今天自提`, recognition, save-and-confirm into the order pool as a formal `ORD-P0-*` order, generated `待出库` fulfillment state, and inventory reservation increase for `30*38*10 / 红色 / 普通提 / 空白袋` from 1320 to 1330, with zero ERP browser error logs. QA screenshot: `screenshots/p0-order-save-state-actions-extracted-smoke.png`.

Latest Stage 2 statement-state update: statement / payment state transitions now have a dedicated helper module in `src/state/officeStatementActions.js`. Mark-sent, payment registration, payment-variance todo creation, variance-handling result updates, write-off blocking, and confirmed-debt / confirmed-write-off status updates are now delegated out of `src/App.jsx`. `App.jsx` keeps modal routing and selected-record lookup, while the statement helper returns updated statement arrays, optional todo payloads, and operator-facing toasts. `npm run build` passes; browser verification at `http://127.0.0.1:5174/` covered 李四电商 `¥108,000` receivable / `¥80,000` received / `¥28,000` variance: write-off blocked before variance handling, payment registration kept `差额待确认`, `未收差额转欠款` moved the record to debt handling, final confirmation set `已确认欠款` while keeping the `¥28,000` amount visible in debt / variance filtering, and no ERP browser error logs. QA screenshot: `screenshots/p0-statement-state-actions-extracted-smoke.png`.

Latest Stage 2 fulfillment-state update: outbound / fulfillment state transitions now have a dedicated helper module in `src/state/officeFulfillmentActions.js`. Print confirmation, express/LTL pickup confirmation, self-pickup / delivery completion, quantity mismatch, unable-to-outbound, and matching office-todo payload creation are no longer embedded directly in the page shell. During browser verification, a stale-selection bug was found: after switching fulfillment tabs, the detail panel could show the first filtered record while action buttons still used the old `selectedFulfillmentId`, causing the print modal to open the wrong customer/order. `FulfillmentPage` now passes the currently displayed fulfillment id into `updateFulfillment`, and `updateFulfillment` applies mutations to that explicit id. `npm run build` passes; browser verification at `http://127.0.0.1:5174/` covered express/LTL custom row `白鲸自营店 / 白印黑 / 白袋黑提` printing without cross-record leakage, printed-to-`待确认拉走`, `确认已拉走` to `已交付`, self-pickup `无法出库` to office todo, nav badge update, and zero ERP browser error logs. QA screenshot: `screenshots/p0-fulfillment-state-actions-extracted-smoke.png`.

Latest Stage 2 state-actions update: the first local state/action extraction pass is complete. Draft-row editing behavior for order entry now lives in `src/state/officeDraftActions.js`, covering field updates, current-row deletion, current-row splitting, and merge-with-next-row transformations. `src/App.jsx` now delegates these draft row transformations to state helpers and remains responsible for React state updates, toasts, and selected-row wiring. This is the first step toward moving local state mutations into a future reducer/service layer. `npm run build` passes; browser verification at `http://127.0.0.1:5174/` covered order-entry product editing, split current row, merge next row, delete current row, status/toast updates, and no ERP browser error logs. QA screenshot: `screenshots/p0-draft-state-actions-extracted-smoke.png`.

Latest Stage 2 domain-rules update: the first domain extraction pass is complete. Order, inventory, fulfillment, statement/payment, todo, status-tone, money-formatting, shorthand label, filter, and display-rule helpers have moved from `src/App.jsx` into `src/domain/officeRules.js`. `src/App.jsx` now imports those rules and keeps only a small P0 fixture bridge for customer-dependent calculations, plus the app shell, local state, and action handlers. This reduces `App.jsx` from 1562 to 1080 lines and prepares a later state/action reducer extraction without changing page behavior. `npm run build` passes; browser verification at `http://127.0.0.1:5174/` covered all six P0 office pages after reload, confirmed each page title and row/detail content rendered, and found no ERP browser error logs. QA screenshot: `screenshots/p0-domain-rules-extracted-smoke.png`.

Latest Stage 2 page-structure update: the six P0 office pages have been extracted from `src/App.jsx` into `src/pages/office/index.jsx`: shared todos, order entry, order pool, inventory lookup, fulfillment, and statements/payments. `src/App.jsx` now keeps the app shell, top bar, modal handling, local state, and a `pageHelpers` bridge for the existing business/display helpers; business rules were intentionally not moved in the same pass. This makes each page independently readable while preserving behavior for the next domain/state extraction step. `npm run build` passes; browser verification at `http://127.0.0.1:5174/` covered all six P0 pages after reload, confirmed each page title and row/detail content rendered, and found no browser error logs. QA screenshot: `screenshots/p0-office-pages-extracted-smoke.png`.

Latest Stage 2 frontend-structure update: the first component-extraction pass is complete. Shared pure UI components `MetricStrip`, `DataTable`, `DetailPane`, `InfoGrid`, `Timeline`, `StatusPill`, and `Segmented` now live in `src/components/ui.jsx`, and `src/App.jsx` imports them instead of keeping their definitions inline. This reduces `App.jsx` by 80 lines without changing business behavior or CSS class contracts, preparing the next page-splitting pass. `npm run build` passes; browser verification at `http://127.0.0.1:5174/` covered all six P0 office pages after reload, confirming navigation, data tables, detail panes, info grids, status pills, metric strips, segmented controls, zero page-level horizontal overflow, and no console errors. QA screenshot: `screenshots/p0-ui-components-refactor-smoke-1440x900.png`.

Latest P0 statement/payment hardening update: the P0 statements / payments page now supports left-side customer search and status filtering for default pending items, this-period statements, debt / variance, payment-confirmation, settled, and all records. The right-side statement detail now shows customer summary, period lines, send status, payment amount, variance/debt amount, and an explicit variance-handling result. Underpaid statements open a variance-handling modal instead of silently changing state; available P0 outcomes are unpaid variance carried to debt, approved rounding / allowance, bill needs recalculation, multiple payments pending, and other. Write-off is now blocked when there is no recorded payment or when a variance exists without a handling result. Choosing unpaid variance carries the amount into debt and keeps the record in debt / variance filtering instead of clearing it. `npm run build` passes; browser verification at `http://127.0.0.1:5174/` covered search/filtering for 李四电商, write-off blocking before variance handling, variance handling to unpaid debt, debt-preserving write-off, 1440x900 and 1024 width no horizontal overflow, and no console errors. QA screenshot: `screenshots/p0-statements-filters-variance-writeoff-1440x900.png`.

Latest P0 inventory lookup hardening update: the P0 inventory page now supports multi-field filtering by size, color, handle, finished-goods style, state, trust level, and free-text search while keeping pending-handling / pending-scrap stock collapsed by default. The list keeps exact inventory-key columns and adds a status/trust view, summary metrics, and a desktop-fitted 12-column inventory table. The detail panel now calculates shortage against an entered customer-needed quantity, shows reference-only similar stock, produces copyable customer wording, and creates a permission-confirmation inventory correction draft without changing inventory totals. `npm run build` passes; browser verification at `http://127.0.0.1:5174/` covered default hidden pending stock, pending-stock expansion/search, shortage calculation for 2000 requested vs 1040 available, reference stock hint, customer wording, correction draft `待确认生效`, no page/table horizontal overflow at 1440x900, and no ERP console errors. QA screenshot: `screenshots/p0-inventory-filters-shortage-correction-1440x900.png`.

Latest P0 order-entry shorthand parsing update: order entry recognition now parses factory custom-print shorthand into structured draft fields instead of leaving it as plain text. Sample recognition covers `白印黑 / 白袋黑提` and `黄印黑 / 黄袋红提`, extracting bag color, print color, handle color, print side, quantity, fulfillment method, and notes where possible. The order-entry detail panel now shows compact color spec, handle color, and notes as editable fields, and formal order lines preserve `handleColor` and note on save. The order-pool detail also surfaces print color, print side, handle color, and notes for later checking. The entry table numeric and amount columns were tightened so `1500`, `3000`, and `¥1,440` remain readable without horizontal overflow. `npm run build` passes; parser checks and browser verification at `http://127.0.0.1:5174/` confirmed six parsed rows, `白印黑 / 白袋黑提`, `黄印黑 / 黄袋红提`, editable detail fields, no page/table horizontal overflow, no ERP console errors, and screenshot evidence. QA screenshot: `screenshots/p0-order-entry-factory-shorthand-1440x900.png`.

Latest machine-counter refinement: the Ounuo bag-making machine counter / daily report has been confirmed as a machine action or cycle count, not qualified finished-goods output. Product requirements now require ERP to label these readings as machine count / cycle count / process telemetry, never as finished quantity, inventory, delivered quantity, chargeable quantity, or payroll piece-count evidence. Daily counter values can be captured from photos, machine daily reports, cumulative reading differences, or manual entry, but they are only compared with qualified bag-making reports, packing counts, inbound records, and abnormal-loss records for process-variance analysis. Remaining on-site verification is per-machine reading type and reset behavior.

Latest P0 fulfillment goods-spec display update: outbound / fulfillment now renders custom products from structured order-line fields instead of a vague goods-summary string. Custom fulfillment rows and package/document previews use product name + size + compact color spec + print side + quantity + notes. Compact color spec supports bag color + print color such as `白印黑`, bag color + handle color such as `白袋黑提` and `黄袋红提`, and combined display such as `白印黑 / 白袋黑提` when both apply. The detail drawer keeps structured bag color / print color / handle color values for checking. The fulfillment detail selection now follows the active tab's filtered rows, so switching from express/LTL to delivery no longer leaves a stale detail record. `npm run build` passes, browser verification at `http://127.0.0.1:5174/` covered express/LTL `白印黑 / 白袋黑提`, delivery `黄印黑 / 黄袋红提`, detail field split, no page-level/table horizontal overflow, screenshot, and console-error checks. QA screenshot: `screenshots/p0-fulfillment-bag-handle-shorthand-1440x900.png`.

Latest Stage 1 order-pool update: the P0 order pool now supports multi-dimensional filters for customer, order status, order type, fulfillment method, exception flag, and statement / debt state. The order detail drawer is split into production / inventory, packing / fulfillment, statement / payment, and flow-summary sections. Shared todos, fulfillment records, and statement records can now deep-link back to the related order line, and the order pool can locate the related fulfillment or statement record. The order-pool list uses compact order-tail / line numbers such as `#010-01` so high-density columns remain readable while full order numbers stay visible in the detail panel and toasts. `npm run build` passes, browser verification at `http://127.0.0.1:5174/` covered custom-print + express/LTL + exception filtering, order-pool-to-fulfillment navigation, fulfillment-to-order navigation, order-pool-to-statement navigation, no page-level horizontal overflow at 1440 and 1024 widths, screenshots, and console-error checks. QA screenshots: `screenshots/p0-order-pool-filters-detail-1440x900.png` and `screenshots/p0-order-pool-cross-navigation-1440x900.png`.

Latest Stage 1 shared-todo update: the P0 shared todo workbench now sorts open todos by urgent / exception / today / latest time / waiting duration, supports `未处理` / `已处理` / `全部` views, and shows todo-type-specific actions instead of one generic action set. Shortage, quantity difference, payment variance, statement, express/LTL pickup, print-label, order-draft, and management-watch todos now display different recommended actions and handling rules. Snooze simulation supports 30 minutes, 2 hours, tomorrow morning, and a specified-time placeholder without changing open todo counts. Print / label todos can be batch-processed as low-risk items, while quantity and money-impacting todos remain one-by-one. `npm run build` passes, browser verification at `http://127.0.0.1:5174/` covered empty handled view, shortage handling, quantity-difference one-by-one actions, snooze state, batch print-label handling, handled view, screenshots, and console-error checks. QA screenshots: `screenshots/p0-todo-type-actions-snooze-1440x900.png` and `screenshots/p0-todo-handled-batch-print-1440x900.png`.

Latest Stage 1 fulfillment update: the P0 outbound / fulfillment page now shows action buttons by delivery method instead of one generic button set. Self-pickup uses `完成自提` and pickup documents, delivery uses `完成送货` and delivery notes, and express/LTL uses package labels followed by `确认已拉走`. Printing express/LTL labels now moves the fulfillment record to `待确认拉走`; only after pickup confirmation does it become `已交付` and enter the reconciliation path. Quantity mismatch and unable-to-outbound both require fixed reason handling and create office todos, while `打开待办` now opens the existing matching todo instead of creating duplicate todos. `npm run build` passes, browser verification at `http://127.0.0.1:5174/` covered express/LTL print-to-pickup, self-pickup completion, quantity mismatch, unable-to-outbound, todo de-duplication, and console-error checks. QA screenshots: `screenshots/p0-fulfillment-method-actions-1440x900.png` and `screenshots/p0-fulfillment-exception-todo-dedupe-1440x900.png`.

Latest code-plan implementation update: Stage 1 order-entry hardening has started according to `docs/development/code-development-plan.zh-CN.md`. The P0 order-entry page now has row-level tools for merging the selected row with the next row, splitting the selected row into two quantity lines, and deleting the selected row. Draft rows now keep visible draft status, support `待确认` fulfillment, and store custom-print detail fields for print color, print side, and artwork status. Saving and confirming now rechecks inventory and price snapshots before formal-order creation, blocks rows with missing order-type-specific fields, and specifically prevents custom printed rows from becoming formal orders until production-critical print fields such as artwork are complete. `npm run build` passes, browser verification at `http://127.0.0.1:5174/` covered split / merge / delete, custom-print missing-artwork blocking, completing artwork then saving to the order pool, and a no-horizontal-overflow layout check for `.entry-table`. QA screenshot: `screenshots/p0-order-entry-row-tools-validation-1440x900.png`.

Latest P0 order-entry display fix: custom printed order lines now show `定制印刷` in the visible order-entry `款式/类型` column instead of looking like plain `空白袋` rows.
